Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the stereochemistry space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 128 and the convergence rate is O(619/n^1).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the stereochemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^128 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 619-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
